# Seed 49 on the AMD Ryzen 7 PRO 8840HS - not small: hypervolume +4.0% (Iteration 288)

`seed 49 tag2 8h then experiment compare-fronts.py --rounds 6 --frontier --cpus
2,8`, commit 22f8b03ab (Iteration 286: the same genes as seed 48 - jitk, lto,
gotodup, compiler, factor - given longer), started 05:28 UTC; the evolution
05:32-11:35 (6 h 03 min - the owner's 8 hours, generations 0-146), the front
measured again by 11:55, the comparison packed by 13:13. 36,868 designs made,
28,225 measured (28,022 alive), 8,643 screened - not measured. Seed 48's packs had
not come back: seed 48 is in this comparison as an archive
(archived-20261014-052835), but has no results file yet.

The comparison's own verdict (five measures): fastest -4.1%, smallest +0.00%,
memory -4 KB, hypervolume +4.0%, best Q -3.9% - **not small**. The Q-best design
of all runs is seed 48's 4599c1f454: 0.157 at 28,117 bytes, 160 KB (Q 0.216 of
s6's); the run's own Q-best 55e68bb8f9, 0.129 at 28,765 bytes, 180 KB (Q 0.204 as
the run measured it). Calibration 0.995 on cpu 2, 1.010 on cpu 8; cpu 2 against
cpu 8 median 1.002, Spearman 1.00.

From the run's database (lab/evolve/seed-facts.py, and the front's genomes):

- **Deaths: 203, all "reach limit at scale 0 - not run"** - none an engine's or
  the corpus's.
- The screen: 8,643 of the 35,309 children from generation 5 skipped (24.5%).
- Children: mutation 55.4%, crossover 33.1%, borrowed 11.5% (the last fifth:
  crossover 36.5%). Via the attic: 5,654 children; 6 of the front.
- The final front, 172 designs: mutation 121, borrowed 24, carried 16, crossover
  11.
- The small third (57 designs, 15,672-17,627 bytes): fsys 3 on 48; klookup on
  none. The fast third (24,722-29,147): fsys 2 on all; klookup on 44.
- The genes seed 48 brought: **factor on 161 of the 172** (level 3, every word, on
  96; 2 on 58; 1 on 7) - in the small third on 56 of 57; **lto on 111 of the 154
  GCC-built**; compiler=1 (clang) on 18; jitk 3 on 23 of the 100 JIT designs;
  gotodup on 24 (12 at 1, 12 at 2).
- The JIT on 99 of the front (jittail on 63, jitos on 21); tail and msc on none.
- In the run: fastest 927064f127 0.128 at 29,147 (184 KB); smallest 5c66ccc371
  15,672 (speed 1.496, 132 KB) - 136 bytes under seed 47's; least memory
  550e367505, 116 KB (16,067 bytes) - the least of any run.
- A try from the front repeats a design the run made 29.3% of the time by
  mutation alone, 0.5% by crossover.

---

# Evolved VM designs

28225 designs evaluated, 28022 alive, 8643 screened - not measured (Iteration 200). Speed is the geometric mean of median cpu time / hand-made s6, long workloads (Iteration 100) over kernel, fib, corpus, loop, bubble, ackermann,
relative to s6-cv8b; size is the self-hosting image, binary the stripped engine. sieve, parse is held out.
The front is over three objectives, equal (Iteration 75): speed, binary + image, peak memory - memory within 8 KB a tie (Iteration 77).


The Q-best design (binary + image x run time x memory - the knee; information): 55e68bb8f9, speed 0.129, binary + image 28,765, memory 180 KB - Q 0.204 of s6-cv8b's.
